package quiz

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"io"
	"log/slog"
	"os"
	"testing"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"
	_ "github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/stdlib"
	"github.com/pressly/goose/v3"

	"github.com/roma007/OpenQuizGo/migrations"
)

func TestTargetDifficulty(t *testing.T) {
	if got := targetDifficulty("low", "g2", nil); got != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("low/g2 = %d, want 1", got)
	}
	if got := targetDifficulty("high", "g8", nil); got != 4 {
		t.Fatalf("high/g8 = %d, want 4", got)
	}
	if got := targetDifficulty("mid", "", map[int]float64{4: 0.7}); got != 4 {
		t.Fatalf("stats target = %d, want 4", got)
	}
}

func TestSelectWithRealPool(t *testing.T) {
	dbURL := os.Getenv("TEST_DATABASE_URL")
	if dbURL == "" {
		t.Skip("TEST_DATABASE_URL not set")
	}
	ctx := context.Background()
	prepareQuizDB(t, dbURL)

	pool, err := pgxpool.New(ctx, dbURL)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("pool: %v", err)
	}
	defer pool.Close()

	childID := seedChild(t, pool)
	offlineID, answeredID := seedQuestions(t, pool, childID)

	svc := New(pool, nil, nil, nil, slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(io.Discard, nil)))
	rule := Rule{
		UnlockMinutes: 20,
		PassThreshold: 3,
		SubjectWeight: map[string]float64{
			"math": 2, "chinese": 1, "science": 1, "fun": 0.5,
		},
		FunRatioCap: 2,
		FunEnabled:  true,
	}

	first, err := svc.Select(ctx, SelectRequest{ChildID: childID, Rule: rule, Seed: 42})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("select: %v", err)
	}
	second, err := svc.Select(ctx, SelectRequest{ChildID: childID, Rule: rule, Seed: 42})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("select again: %v", err)
	}
	if len(first.Selected) != len(second.Selected) {
		t.Fatalf("non-deterministic: %d vs %d", len(first.Selected), len(second.Selected))
	}
	for i := range first.Selected {
		if first.Selected[i].QuestionID != second.Selected[i].QuestionID {
			t.Fatal("same seed produced different question order")
		}
	}

	if first.Total < 3 || first.Total > 12 {
		t.Fatalf("total = %d, want 3..12", first.Total)
	}
	if first.MaxScore < rule.PassThreshold {
		t.Fatalf("max score = %.1f < threshold", first.MaxScore)
	}
	funCount := 0
	for _, item := range first.Selected {
		if item.Subject == "fun" {
			funCount++
		}
		if item.QuestionID == offlineID {
			t.Fatal("offline question was selected")
		}
		if item.QuestionID == answeredID {
			t.Fatal("recently answered question was selected")
		}
	}
	if funCount > rule.FunRatioCap {
		t.Fatalf("fun count = %d, cap = %d", funCount, rule.FunRatioCap)
	}

	for seed := int64(1); seed <= 20; seed++ {
		result, err := svc.Select(ctx, SelectRequest{ChildID: childID, Rule: rule, Seed: seed})
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("seed %d: %v", seed, err)
		}
		for _, item := range result.Selected {
			if item.QuestionID == offlineID {
				t.Fatalf("seed %d selected offline question", seed)
			}
			if item.QuestionID == answeredID {
				t.Fatalf("seed %d selected recently answered question", seed)
			}
			if item.Repeated {
				t.Fatalf("seed %d unexpectedly used repeated question", seed)
			}
		}
	}

	emptyRule := Rule{
		UnlockMinutes: 20,
		PassThreshold: 3,
		SubjectWeight: map[string]float64{"english": 2},
		DailyLimit:    60,
		Timezone:      "Asia/Shanghai",

+// applyQuestionCount 应用精确题量：家长配置了题量时按配置出题，
+// 否则保留 MaxQuestions 的“上限”语义与非电视设备的推导题量（082）。
+func applyQuestionCount(req SelectRequest, derived int) int {
+	if req.QuestionCount > 0 {
+		n := req.QuestionCount
+		if n > rule.MaxSessionQuestions {
+			n = rule.MaxSessionQuestions
+		}
+		if n < 1 {
+			n = 1
+		}
+		return n
+	}
+	if req.MaxQuestions > 0 && req.MaxQuestions < derived {
+		return max(3, req.MaxQuestions)
+	}
+	return derived
+}

Issue in ContextForDevice: `base` may be zero Rule{} when child rule missing (err ignored: `base, _, _ := s.repo.getForChild`). applyTVConfig(base, cfg) with empty weights → MaxScore = 0 → effective threshold returns cfg.PassThreshold. Fine.

But another subtlety: ContextForDevice effective threshold uses applyTVConfig which sets DailyLimit etc.; passing base rule unchanged to DayBounds. Fine.

Potential inconsistency: Select validates `req.Rule` with `rule.Validate`, which uses SessionSize(rule) (n≥5) and threshold now clamped to MaxScore(questionCount). MaxScore(questionCount) may be less than threshold? Clamping ensures ≤. But Validate compares threshold against maxScore at derived n (≥5), which is ≥ MaxScore(n) for n<5? Not necessarily monotonic due to fun allocation, but generally larger n yields ≥ score. For n=1 threshold=2 (math), derived n=5 max = 7 ≥2 fine. For count=1 threshold=maxWeight e.g. 2 and fun enabled cap allocates 2 fun slots at n=5 → non-fun slots 3 → 6 + 1 = 7 fine.

Now check selector's `MaxQuestions` compatibility; fine.

One more important path: watch grant issuance uses rule snapshot DailyLimit etc. unchanged.

Now the `context` field `question_count` won't be parsed by TV client (Gson ignores). Fine.

Next: parent UI test for settings page? Not necessary but maybe add? Skip.

Now update watch tests? Not affected.

Run full go test (skips DB) and build. Also `make test` maybe runs all with -race. Let's run `make test` to be safe.

| 项目 | 内容 |
| --- | --- |

## 1. 目标与范围

- 交付目标：家长端按电视配置每次答题题量（1–5 题，默认 1）；服务端按配置精确组卷，并保证门槛可达；存量电视保留原题量。
- 范围边界：
  - 包含：迁移、`TVConfig` 字段与校验、组卷题量、门槛保底、家长端设置项、接口契约、测试。

Now double-check the test DB migration state and that `make migrate-up` works (uses DATABASE_URL from .env maybe). We already migrated test DB to 34 via tests. Local dev DB? Not necessary.

Potential issue: `identity` package's tv device queries use tvColumns with new field; tests for identity TV login might construct config? Make test passed.

Potential issue: parent `TvConfig` required param added — other constructions only test file; done.

Now think about the semantics "每次答题题量" and the pass threshold clamping. There's a subtle bug: `rule.Validate(req.Rule)` in Select uses `SessionSize` and theoreticalMax at derived n, requiring threshold ≤ maxScore(derived). Our clamped threshold is ≤ maxScore(questionCount). Is maxScore monotonic in n? Consider n=1: funSlots=0 → maxScore = maxWeight. n=5: funSlots=2 → e.g. 3 highest + 2 fun. Since non-fun slots = n - funSlots for n=5 is 3 ≥ 1, score ≥ maxWeight + fun... monotonic enough. n=2: funSlots=1, non-fun 1 = maxWeight + fun. n=3: 1 + 2fun. n=4: 2max+2fun. Generally increasing. So Validate passes.

Another important check: for n=1 and `Random=true` (TV default quiz_scope=random), `selectRandom` picks random candidates of any subject, not necessarily highest weight. If it picks a chinese question (weight 1) while threshold clamped to maxWeight 1.5, score 1 < 1.5 → session's passThreshold gets further clamped by our CreateSession fallback (result.MaxScore = 1) → fine! Good, that's exactly why the final clamp matters. But note `selectRandom` also has score check? It doesn't check score vs threshold (only the non-random path does). Great.

Wait: in selectRandom, `rule.Validate(req.Rule)` requires threshold ≤ maxScore(SessionSize) which is fine.

So with 1 random question, threshold becomes actual question weight, requiring correct answer. Good.

Another subtle issue: `CreateSession` passThreshold fallback also affects topics and existing behavior positively.

Now, `ContextForDevice` effective threshold uses theoretical max, could differ from actual session threshold for random picks. TV displays context threshold? TV status page maybe shows? Not important.

What about `rule.PassThreshold` stored in tv_config still 3; when question_count=1, every session threshold ~1–2. Fine.

Also: parent TV settings page has no UI for pass threshold, so no confusion.

Next: update data-model doc? docs/data-model.md may list tv_config; check.

已完成「家长端配置每次答题题量」的设计、实施与测试。

## 实现内容

**配置项（按电视独立）**
- 家长端 → 电视列表 → 某台电视设置 → 「做题」卡片新增 **每次答题题量**，可选 **1–5 题**
- 新电视默认 **1 题**；已存在的电视迁移时回填 **5 题**（升级前 `SessionSize` 推导下限就是 5 题，保持最接近的行为）
- 旧版家长端保存其他设置时未带该字段，服务端沿用原值，不会被重置为 1

**服务端**
- 迁移 `migrations/00034_tv_config_question_count.sql`：`tv_config.question_count`，约束 1–5
- `/v1/family/tvs*` 配置读写、`/v1/tv/context` 均返回 `question_count`
- 组卷严格按配置题量出题（不再强制至少 3 题）
- 题量变小导致原通过门槛不可达时，**实际门槛自动下调到该题量可拿到的最高分**，避免孩子永远答不过（例如 1 题时答对即可通过）

**接口契约**
- `api/openapi.yaml` 已同步 `question_count` 与门槛说明

## 验证结果
- `make test` 全绿（含新增题量校验、门槛保底、精确题量测试）
- `make vet` 通过
- 家长端 `dart analyze` 无问题，`flutter test` 60 项全过
- 迁移在测试库 goose 升级到 34 成功

## 文档与归档
- 设计：`docs/design/082-家长端配置每次答题题量.md`
- 计划：`docs/plans/082-家长端配置每次答题题量实施计划.md`
- 已同步 `docs/ROADMAP.md`、`docs/README.md`
- 对话已归档：`docs/conversations/2026-11-15-002-家长端每次答题题量配置.md`

尚未提交 Git（提交时 pre-commit 会自动把版本从 1.0.33 升到 1.0.34）。发布时 `00034` 迁移需按流程先备份数据库。

```text
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# OpenQuizGo 一键发布（对应设计/计划 078）
#
# 解决「提交即升版、服务器 .env 不自动同步」的版本真空期：
# 把「备份 → 打回滚点 → 同步 → nginx/units → 构建 → 写版本 → 迁移+重启 → 自检 → 登记」
# 固化成一条命令。构建成功后才写 .env，构建失败不会污染版本号。
#
# 跨项目协作（AGENTS.md §9）：开头读 /srv/ops 的 inbox，有 open 请求就 exit 2，
# 除非显式 --ack-open；收尾自动登记 /srv/ops/state/quizgo.json 与 journal。
#
# 用法：
#   scripts/deploy.sh                    # 标准发布
#   scripts/deploy.sh --with-admin-web   # 管理端前端有改动时，附带同步 admin-web/dist
#   scripts/deploy.sh --with-nginx       # deploy/nginx 有改动时，同步配置 + nginx -t + reload
#   scripts/deploy.sh --with-units       # deploy/systemd 有改动时，重装 unit 并重启 timer
#   scripts/deploy.sh --skip-backup      # 仅跳过备份（服务端代码/迁移没变时可用，有硬保护）
#   scripts/deploy.sh --skip-build       # 不重建镜像，只对齐版本号（服务端代码/迁移没变时可用，有硬保护）
#   scripts/deploy.sh --ack-open         # 跳过 inbox 闸门（已确认 open 请求与本次发布无关，或本次就是处理它）
#   scripts/deploy.sh --dry-run          # 只打印将执行的命令
#
# --skip-build / --skip-backup 的硬保护：以服务器上的 /srv/quizgo/.deployed-image
# （记录上次构建的 commit 与镜像 ID）为基线，若服务端代码或迁移有变化就直接拒绝，
# 避免出现「报新版本、实际跑旧代码」。
#
# 环境变量：SSH_HOST(默认 quizgo) REMOTE_DIR(默认 /srv/quizgo) BASE_URL(默认 http://127.0.0.1:8082)
set -euo pipefail

ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
cd "$ROOT"

SSH_HOST="${SSH_HOST:-quizgo}"
REMOTE_DIR="${REMOTE_DIR:-/srv/quizgo}"
BASE_URL="${BASE_URL:-http://127.0.0.1:8082}"
MARKER_NAME=".deployed-image"

WITH_ADMIN_WEB=0
WITH_NGINX=0
WITH_UNITS=0
SKIP_BACKUP=0
SKIP_BUILD=0
ACK_OPEN=0
DRY_RUN=0

for arg in "$@"; do
  case "$arg" in
    --with-admin-web) WITH_ADMIN_WEB=1 ;;
    --with-nginx)     WITH_NGINX=1 ;;
    --with-units)     WITH_UNITS=1 ;;
    --skip-backup)    SKIP_BACKUP=1 ;;
    --skip-build)     SKIP_BUILD=1 ;;
    --ack-open)       ACK_OPEN=1 ;;
    --dry-run)        DRY_RUN=1 ;;
    -h|--help)
      awk 'NR>1 && /^#/ { sub(/^# ?/, ""); print; next } NR>1 { exit }' "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}"
      exit 0 ;;
    *) echo "未知参数：${arg}（--help 查看用法）" >&2; exit 2 ;;
  esac
done

VERSION="$(tr -d '[:space:]' < VERSION)"

```
